## Simpler confirmations: three tiers, no typed words (2026-11-14)

**What changed.** Board "Confirmations and deletes" built across the app (see SYSTEM.md "Simpler confirmations"). Shared pieces: `components/v2/ui/confirm-dialog.tsx` (`EConfirmDialog`, tiers ordinary / destructive / sensitive, reason, Add details, error, phone sheet), `components/v2/ui/reason-picker.tsx`, `components/v2/ui/undo-toast.tsx`, `components/v2/shared/own-pin-fields.tsx` (the one credential field, five states), `lib/confirmations/reasons.ts` (plain module), `lib/security/route-verification.ts`. `EConfirmModal` keeps its props and renders the new dialog; `confirmPhrase` is ignored. No schema change, no migration.

**Defaults applied, pending owner confirmation.** (1) With no PIN the account password alone confirms; never when a PIN exists; lockout, Test-as refusal and rate limits unchanged; `SENSITIVE_ACTION_CONFIRMED` audit names the credential. (2) Invoice and credit-note void require a quick-pick reason (`voidReason`, `CLIENT_INVOICE_VOIDED` audit; no record or amount changes). (3) Draft invoice line removal: no PIN, tier B, Undo for plain manual lines; past DRAFT unchanged; the draft status is rechecked under the row lock. (4) Cleaner submitted-invoice delete is tier C (`cleaner_invoices.delete`, Payroll Manage tier so rights are unchanged). (5) Quick picks fixed in the product.

**Server rule changes.** Reasons without minimums: reconcile (min 10 → 1), laundry void (3 → 1), damage void (10 → 1), delivery review evidence (10 → 1), draft evidence review (10 → 1). Newly required: invoice/credit-note `voidReason`, laundry undo-void `reason`, approvals history `note` for edit and delete. PIN now verified by the action's route: quote extras DELETE, checklist module DELETE, shopping-run PATCH that removes a receipt, va-teams team DELETE and member DELETE, cleaner invoice DELETE (new), damage VOID (new). AI credentials route: `security: { pin } | { password }` through `verifySensitiveAction` (legacy top-level `password` read as `security.password`). `GET /api/me/admin-pin` adds `attemptsLeft`, `timezone`, and `code: ACTUAL_ACTOR_REQUIRED` on its testing refusal. The Xero reconcile route still requires the correction reference (3+ characters), so the dialog keeps it visible and required.

**Inventory (board rows → final state).**

| Dialog | Final | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Reconcile Xero invoice | Done, C + reason | Quick pick; reference stays visible and required (server requires it); note under Add details; no VOID |

## Xero stays connected (2026-11-15)

Owner report: "it only connects for 30 mins from xero." Section: `docs/SYSTEM.md` › "Xero connection stays connected (2026-11-15)".

**Cause, proved before the fix** (`tests/lib/xero-token-lifecycle.test.ts` first block, run against the old `lib/xero/client.ts`: 3 of 3 failed — two concurrent calls made 2 token exchanges; a 503 renewal returned "No active Xero connection" and stored "SIGN_IN_LAPSED"; a 401 was thrown, not renewed and retried). Also: connections made before `offline_access` (2026-11-11) have no refresh token; no keep-alive for the 60-day unused expiry; tokens plaintext; status said "Connected" for a lapsed connection.

**Fix.** `lib/xero/token-service.ts` (single-flight lease + compare-and-set on `tokenVersion`, encrypted persist before use, transient vs terminal classification, tenant check), `lib/xero/keepalive.ts` + worker job `xero-token-keepalive` (daily 03:47 Sydney), `lib/xero/connection-status.ts`, `lib/xero/reconnect-notice.ts` (catalog `xero.reconnect_needed`, inbox only, administrators, once per outage), `lib/xero/credentials.ts`; `lib/xero/client.ts` routes every call through one `xeroCall` (401 → renew once → retry once). Settings › Xero card `components/v2/admin/settings/xero-connection-card.tsx`. Migration `20261115050000_xero_token_lifecycle`. Not changed: push payloads, money arithmetic, issued/paid records; failed pushes stay retryable through the existing Retry paths with their idempotency keys.

**Verification.**
- `npx prisma migrate deploy` on fresh `xero_token_fresh` and `xero_token_fresh2`, on a database at the previous migration with a legacy no-refresh-token row and a renewable row (`xero_token_backfill`: backfill gave NEEDS_RECONNECT/NO_REFRESH_TOKEN and CONNECTED with `connectedAt`/`lastRefreshedAt` copied), and on `sneek_ui`; repeated: "No pending migrations to apply"; `prisma migrate diff --from-url … --to-schema-datamodel prisma/schema.prisma --exit-code` exit 0 on each. Note: deploy also applied another agent's pending `20261115060000_sending_safeguards` to `sneek_ui`.
- Tests (Xero mocked; no credentials here): `tests/lib/xero-token-lifecycle.test.ts` 19, `tests/db/xero-token-refresh.test.ts` 4 (PostgreSQL 55439, fresh database, two Prisma clients as two processes, `--no-file-parallelism`), `tests/lib/xero-reconnect-notice.test.ts` 3, `tests/components/xero-connection-card.test.tsx` 5, `tests/lib/xero-connect-exchange.test.ts` 3; existing Xero/finance/notification/worker suites (35 files, 356 tests) pass except `notification-catalog` "lists every file that sends a notification", which fails on `lib/maintenance/assign-notice.ts` (another agent's in-progress file, not this change). Changed on purpose: `tests/lib/finance-decisions-rules.test.ts` (sign-in wording and the NEEDS_RECONNECT rule), `tests/lib/xero-read-transport.test.ts` (mock gains `updateMany`/notice).
- `NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=6144 npx tsc --noEmit -p .`: clean on the final run (an earlier run showed one error in `lib/ops/tomorrow-prep.ts` from another agent's in-progress work, since resolved).

## Website, blog and marketing redesign (2026-11-16, boards "Website and blog" and "Marketing")

**What changed.** No schema change. Website › Pages is a three-pane editor (sections with unsaved counts, form with changed fields outlined and "changed · was …", a preview pane fed the unsaved draft through the existing `/v2/admin/website/preview` renderer) with one "Save and publish" / "Discard…" bar; publishing sends `websiteRevision` and the settings route refuses a stale draft (409 `website_changed`, nothing saved, draft kept, Compare / Keep mine / Load the published version) and a website change that leaves an image without alt text (400 `alt_text_missing`). The guard runs inside the settings write lock (`saveAppSettings(input, { guard })`). Website › Blog is a list with the post editor beside it (search-result preview, excerpt count, tags, cover and gallery, publish time in organisation time) and real scheduling: `publicBlogWhere(now)` on every public read (blog list, post page and metadata, home page latest posts, admin site preview, sitemap, Growth count); `/blog`, `/blog/[slug]`, `/v2/blog`, `/v2/blog/[slug]` and the sitemap render per request (no static params / revalidate). Marketing is one workspace with five tabs; the campaign editor is who / what / when with audience counts beside each option and skipped counts (unsubscribed, bounce list, no email) before sending (`POST /api/admin/email-campaigns/reach`), tier A schedule / send naming the count, "Retry the N" (`POST /api/admin/email-campaigns/[id]/retry`), "not recorded" until the email service reports; the legacy sender keeps FAILED rows (with the reason) and SKIPPED rows instead of deleting claims. Social posts never claim sNeek posted or reminds. Discount codes and plans are tables; images show where they are used (`GET /api/admin/marketing/assets/[id]/usage`) before tier B removal. `middleware.ts` allows same-origin framing for the preview route only.

**Owner defaults (pending owner confirmation).** Publish straight away (restore from Settings › audit); alt text required; operations managers as today (website pages read only; blog, marketing full); no SMS campaigns; no direct social posting; a test send is recommended, not required.

**Not built as drawn.** Social "Remind at your time": sNeek has no reminder for social posts, so the step reads "Plan · a time, shown here" and the page says no reminders are sent (building one would need the notification intent pipeline and an owner decision). The email layout column reuses the existing block editor (a list of blocks with settings) rather than the drawn canvas; the board's "Templates and images" summary card is the existing Templates and Images tabs. Field-level "changed" outlines are on Home (hero, figures, folding sub-sections) and every image field; other sections show their unsaved count in the section list and the bar's summary. Header actions wrap under the description at 1280 (shared page header). Preview pane renders the Estate preview renderer, which is a faithful look-alike of the public home page, not the public page itself (as before).

**Tests.** New: `tests/lib/blog-scheduling.test.ts`, `tests/lib/website-draft.test.ts`, `tests/api/website-publish-guard.test.ts`, `tests/components/website-blog-editor.test.tsx`, `tests/lib/campaign-reach-retry.test.ts`, `tests/api/marketing-website-route-guards.test.ts`, `tests/components/marketing-workspace.test.tsx`, `tests/lib/website-preview-frame.test.ts`; rewritten for the new UI: `tests/components/social-manager-ai.test.tsx` and the v2 cases of `tests/components/campaign-single-recipient.test.tsx`. 31 files / 367 tests passed with the required scans (server-client values, left accents, ops policy, email HTML scan, tutorials) and the existing campaign / segment / unsubscribe / growth / middleware suites. ## Everyday dialogs and sheets (2026-11-16)

**Built.** Board "Everyday dialogs and sheets" (see `docs/SYSTEM.md` › "Everyday dialogs and sheets"). One shared assign dialog (`components/v2/admin/jobs/assign-dialog.tsx`) used by Jobs quick assign, the Jobs bulk bar, the job page assign panel and the Calendar, with the same request bodies as before; leave and expired police checks / visas read first from the new read-only `GET /api/admin/jobs/assign-facts` and shown in the list, "Assign anyway?" asked before saving, the server's 409 still handled and the override still audited (`ASSIGNMENT_WARNING_OVERRIDDEN`, server unchanged). Move or reassign as one window (desktop dialog, phone sheet, drag confirmation) with "Tell the client?" (Yes for date/time changes, No for cleaner swaps, email only, explicit every time; optional `notifyClient` on the reschedule apply route, prepared cleaner-change email sent unedited through the existing client communications route on Yes). Add an extra window with "Email the client the new total", unticked (route's existing `notifyClient`). Record payment with money facts first and a plain statement that nothing is sent to Xero. Message team, Report a stock need, Log a found item (guest shown before saving) and New case (three-way client visibility) on the shared form layout. Shared components extended only additively: `EActionDialog` props `layout`, `phoneTitle`, `footerNote`; new parts module `components/v2/ui/form-dialog-parts.tsx`.

**Rail showing only "My profile".** Reproduced on the current tree as admin and as ops manager on Command, Jobs, People, Hiring and Settings: every full page load showed only "My profile" for 1.5–9 s (dev) until `/api/me/ops-permissions` answered, and permanently when that read failed (admin included). Cause: `OpsAccessProvider` treated "no answer yet" as fail-closed. Fixed with a server-resolved seed from the root layout (`lib/rbac/ops-navigation-seed.ts`; none during test-as or in a retained-account context) and keeping the last verified answer on a failed refresh. After the fix: 39 entries (admin) / 36 (ops) from the first sample on all five pages, also with the permissions read forced to fail. Regression tests `tests/components/ops-access-seed.test.tsx`, `tests/lib/ops-navigation-seed.test.ts`.

**Not built as drawn.** Record payment: the board's line "does not ... send a receipt" is untrue (a recorded payment triggers the existing receipt email), so the dialog says the receipt goes as before. Add an extra: "New fixed price $305.00" is not computed in the browser (no server preview; AGENTS forbids UI financial arithmetic), shown as "Fixed price now" plus "the new total shows once it is saved". Assign: distance ("4 km") not shown (no read-only distance for staffed jobs); "Set primary" kept. Move phone sheet: keeps the cleaner list with Free/Clash/Leave flags and "Assign QA inspector" instead of a single select, and its × close (EBottomSheet). Bulk reschedule keeps the automatic client email (no choice offered there). Case labels keep the product's existing words (Lost and found, Ops, Critical). Idempotency: only Message team and urgent stock routes accept a request key; assign, payment, extras, found item and case have none, so their retry is guarded by frozen fields and kept drafts, not by a server key. Message team keeps the shared Send by component.

**Tutorials.** Jobs tour (`admin.jobs`) to version 2, step "unassigned" What's new; release note `2026.11.16-everyday-dialogs`; fingerprints refreshed with `scripts/tutorials/update-fingerprints.ts`. No `data-tour` attribute was on a replaced control.

## Two-step backup codes and QA / Maintenance page tours (2026-11-17)

**Built.** See `docs/SYSTEM.md` › Your profile › "Two-step backup codes" and › In-app tutorials › "QA and Maintenance page tours". Backup codes: table `TwoFactorBackupCode` (migration `20261117010000_two_factor_backup_codes`, additive; older sha256 codes copied as `sha256-legacy` rows with no invented date, old column cleared), keyed-hash storage, single use by one conditional update, ten codes on enrolment / method switch / "Make new backup codes…" (tier C through `EConfirmDialog` + `verifySensitiveAction`, refused while testing as someone), one-time display with Copy, Download .txt and Print, "Use a backup code" at sign-in on the shared `2fa:<email>` lockout with "Backup code accepted · N left" and a prompt at three or fewer, inbox + email notice through the intent pipeline with explicit transports (`account.backup_code_used`, new scope `ACCOUNT_HOLDER`, block design `twoFactorBackupCodeUsed`, wording "Backup code used"), deletion on turn-off / recovery link / administrator reset, audit of every creation, use, failure and deletion without the code. Tours: `qa.today` (5 steps), `qa.inspection` (7), `qa.reviews` (3), `maintenance.today` (4), `maintenance.tickets` (4), `maintenance.visit` (8), all version 1, release note `2026.11.17-qa-maintenance-tours`, fingerprints from the script (welcome.qa / welcome.maintenance recorded "no visible change"). Running-clock markers: QA on-site timer and maintenance clocked-in visit (`data-tour-busy="clock"`), QA check-in / monitoring (`"check-in"`).

**Found and fixed.** (1) Pre-existing: the two-step proof cookie was parsed by splitting on every dot, so every real address failed sign-in after a correct second step (only visible in a real browser; unit tests mocked it). Fixed in `verifyTwoFaOk`, regression test added. (2) The admin welcome opened over the one-time codes on a new administrator's profile; the display is now a busy marker. (3) The two-step card blanked to "Loading…" on its refresh after a change, hiding new codes for a moment; a refresh now keeps the card.

**Deviations / not built.** Accept, decline and "Other price" exist only on Maintenance Today's offer card, so they are in the `maintenance.today` tour, not the ticket tour; the ticket tour covers the visit (no "request a higher price" control exists on the ticket page). The QA inspection steps for the cleaner's photos and rework live inside tabs; when their tab is not open they are skipped and recorded as missing, as the engine does for any hidden control. The QA inspection tour waits while the on-site timer runs or the check-in step is shown, so in practice it starts once the timer is paused or the inspection is submitted. No stand-alone "Make new backup codes" button outside Your profile (cleaner, client, laundry, QA, maintenance profile screens still show codes once at enrolment; the route accepts their password). QA practice mode not built (out of scope). No "QA Stats" tour (its page reads failed counts as 0 — left unchanged, outside scope).

A database at the previous migration with fixture rows (valid JSON set, unreadable JSON, two-step off) was upgraded: two `sha256-legacy` rows with null `createdAt`, the unreadable row left untouched, the off row cleared; repeat and diff clean. Later in the session other work added `20261117020000/020100_social_*` migrations to the tree; a second fresh database with every migration and `sneek_ui` both diff clean against the current `schema.prisma`. `npx prisma generate` run.

## Route map, Shelf labels and Cleaner home addresses (2026-11-17)

**Built.** Boards "Route map" (with phone frames), "Shelf labels", "Cleaner home addresses" (see `docs/SYSTEM.md` › "Route map, Shelf labels and Cleaner home addresses"). **Open owner questions.** Route order groups by suburb before start time when the rule is On, which can place an earlier job later in the list; and stops beyond the rule's limit are not shown. Both are existing rules and were left unchanged; they can be changed in Settings › Scheduling rules.

**Correction (coordinator, same day).** The first version changed what the Scheduling rules "Routes: group by suburb" and "Routes: stops shown" do (start time first, every stop shown) and reworded them in the register. That conflicted with the standing owner instruction not to change any rules, so it was reverted: `lib/scheduling-rules/registry.ts` equals origin/main and `buildDailyRoutePlan` returns exactly origin/main's output for both settings (new test). Kept: the card and map key say "N more stops are not shown — the rule 'Routes: stops shown' is set to M" with a link to Settings › Scheduling rules; Google Maps links say which stops they cover; "Out of time order" is worded as information ("Grouped by suburb: a 10:00 stop comes after a 13:00 stop") with the same link.

**Not built as drawn.** No "Placed, west of this map" or edge arrow: the Google map fits every pin, so nothing is outside it. The board's "Proposed: start time first" note is replaced by information linking to the rule (rules unchanged); a "Times overlap" flag was added. The Order rule tile links to Settings › Scheduling rules (where the route settings now live), not "Route optimisation". Suburb centres come from the properties sNeek looks after in that suburb (no suburb boundary data or geocoder call); a home in a suburb with no property is drawn on a ~2 km grid square ("Placed, shown by area"). The assign dialog does not re-check "assigned elsewhere meanwhile" (the server's existing checks are leave and documents only). Page-level sticky does not work under the portal's global `overflow-x: hidden`, so from 1024px the route list scrolls in its own region beside the map. The classic `/admin/jobs/route-map` page keeps its single 10-stop link. The browser screenshots were taken before the correction (they show the start-time order and the every-stop notice); the corrected wording is covered by component tests but was not re-screenshotted. Phone fact tiles show the board's three (nobody, assigned, driving); route count is in the description and the order rule is one line.

## Pay record pages: shopping run, pay run, cleaner invoice (2026-11-17)

**Built.** Boards "Shopping run", "Pay run", "Cleaner invoice" and "Pay run and cleaner invoice, phone" (see `docs/SYSTEM.md` › "Pay record pages"). Shopping run: reimbursement band and approval as the primary action, run status in More, item lines with costs as entered, charges to clients in tone bands, payment and receipts, reimbursement PDFs, purchase order, history from the run's own dates, tier A stock add, phone Summary / Items / Receipts. Pay run: stage bar, stored totals, payouts with problem flags and a collapsed, lazily read breakdown (jobs with stored assignment rate snapshots, settled shopping, adjustments, inspections), tier C Process and run status, tier A Mark paid and payout status, Failed → Set back to Confirmed…, phone money card, payout sheet and docked step. Cleaner invoice: pay-rule band from stored requested/approved amounts (client charge read only for finance viewers), lines with rate snapshots, comparison, Xero card, status notices, Approve tier A, phone Lines / Compare / History.

**Server rule changes.** `processPayrollRun` claims the run (CONFIRMED → PROCESSING) and each Stripe payout before money moves; 409 for a second request or a non-Confirmed run. Process: ADMIN + own PIN (`payroll.process`) in the same request, refused while testing as someone, audited. Run-wide status: ADMIN only, reason required, own PIN (`payroll.run_status`), `expectedStatus`, no-op when unchanged. Payout status: ADMIN or OPS_MANAGER, guarded update with `expectedPayoutStatus`, no-op when unchanged. Shopping reimbursement approval (PENDING_APPROVAL → payable): approver = actual actor, receipt or typed `approvalOverrideNote` (409 `RECEIPT_REQUIRED`), one `SHOPPING_REIMBURSEMENT_APPROVED` audit under the run lock. Cleaner invoice Xero push failures now record `CLEANER_INVOICE_XERO_PUSH_FAILED` with the error (recording only). Callers updated: classic `/admin/payroll/[id]` (PIN and reason dialogs, no Process on Failed), classic shopping-runs workspace (asks for the note on `RECEIPT_REQUIRED`), the cleaner-invoice list (Approve opens the same tier A dialog).

**Owner defaults (pending owner confirmation).** Shopping: approval allowed with an uncosted bought line, for the costed amount, naming the line; receipt required with an audited typed-note override; operations managers keep approval. Pay run: processing not blocked by missing bank details (payout stays Pending and is named); operations managers mark single payouts, run-wide status is ADMIN only; jobs collapsed by default. Phone: sending payouts allowed with the PIN; the invoice opens on Lines.

**Not built as drawn.** Pay run per-job Hours/Amount columns and the per-payout Job pay column: the pay-run rail stores totals per person, not per job, so showing them would recompute pay; rows show the stored rate snapshot and stored totals instead. "Stripe set-up not finished" can only be detected when a Stripe payout has no account id; account readiness is never checked against Stripe (no call), so a payout with an account reads "account on file", and a server with no Stripe key is flagged. Shopping "Add to stock" says purchases go into the buyer's stock on hand (what the service does), not "each property's count goes up". Receipt amounts are not read or stored, so receipts say "amount not recorded". Phone charges-to-clients sheets per property were not built; the same card list shows on the Summary tab. The board's "new" markers were not shipped. The "Save failed" status draft is covered by a component path, not induced in the browser.

**Fixed on the way.** Pay run period and payslip link: `createPayrollRun` stores `+10:00` boundaries, so reading them in daylight time shifted the end a day and the payslip link sliced the UTC instant (a day early); the page now reads the stored calendar days at +10:00 for the title and payslip link. Missing shopping costs printed as $0.00 on the run PDF and client reimbursement PDF; they now print "Not entered" (totals unchanged).

**Tests.** New: `tests/lib/pay-records-rules.test.ts` (10), `tests/components/pay-records-pages.test.tsx` (18), `tests/db/pay-records-money.test.ts` (10, real PostgreSQL on a fresh `payrec_fresh`, `--no-file-parallelism`, real concurrency: process twice → one 200/one 409/one audit; mark paid twice → one audit; approve invoice twice → one audit; shopping approval twice → one audit for the costed amount; impersonation, ops and wrong PIN refused; Failed run handling; breakdown reads the stored snapshot). Updated: `tests/components/cleaner-invoice-review.test.tsx`, `tests/components/shopping-client-charges.test.tsx`. Ran 59 files / 831 tests (every payroll, cleaner-invoice, shopping and finance suite plus server-client-value-imports, no-left-accent-lines, ops-policy, access-parity, email-html-scan, tutorials-up-to-date): all pass; DB suite 10/10 (run three times). `tests/api/ai-settings-routes.test.ts` fails one assertion (AI feature count 9 vs 8) from the parallel social-generator work, not this change. TypeScript (`npx tsc --noEmit -p .`): passes with no errors on the final tree. No schema change, so no migration. No tour on these pages; `tutorials-up-to-date` passes unchanged.

**Inspected (Chromium 1243, `next dev` on 3231 against `sneek_ui`, 390 and 1280, light and dark; stopped, `.next-pay` removed, `tsconfig.json` restored).** Fixtures (tag `pfx-`): the pay run made by `createPayrollRun` + `confirmPayrollRun`, a FAILED run fixture, eight cleaner invoice states, a shopping run with a missing cost and a receipt (client charges by `ensureShoppingClientChargesForRun`); all removed afterwards; no Stripe or Xero call. Screenshots in the scratchpad `gapA2` folder (124): each page, the invoice's eight states, the dialogs (process, run status, set back, mark paid, approve, reverse, delete, reject claim, shopping approve, stock, remove receipt), More menus, the phone tabs, payout sheet and docked steps, and an operations manager view. Fixed after looking: table widths at 1280, failed payout reason first, period day shift, "Why, required, required", nowrap links, phone money card, grouping by property. No horizontal page overflow at 390. The admin has no PIN on `sneek_ui`, so the tier C dialogs show the password state. One page error seen once during an HMR reload while editing.

**Owner defaults (pending owner confirmation).** No second PIN before full access; one-hour limit kept; the person is told only after full access, by an inbox note (`account.tested_as`, once per session, who and when only); administrators named for office staff only; Ask is inbox only, once per person per area per organisation day; office phone shown on portal pages (and "not set" said when empty).

The Test-as "Who can change it" for the Test as page names the grantable capability (rules allow granting Test as) instead of "only an administrator can make you an administrator".

**Fixed on the way.** (1) `middleware.ts` rebuilt the request without its method on the edge runtime, so every request looked like GET to the view-only check: view-only writes were refused only by routes with their own guard. Now `new NextRequest(original, { headers, method: original.method })`; seen in the browser: PATCH/POST during a view-only test now answer 403 `IMPERSONATION_READ_ONLY` (before: 405/409 from the handlers). This restores the documented rule; it is a behaviour change worth the owner's attention. (2) The banner's `.sticky.top-0` offset opened a gap above every portal header (sticky never sticks here because html/body clip overflow); removed. (3) Breadcrumb on denial pages read "Details"; now "Access".

**Tests.** New: `tests/lib/access-denial.test.ts` (35: return-path acceptance and 14 open-redirect/injection/loop refusals, display shortening, page names tied to the admin rail source, office wording incl. preset/own-setting/assistant, Ask not offered while testing or for role/unmapped, portal wording per portal, no administrator names on portal pages, missing phone, testing footnote and no sign-out, unknown state), `tests/lib/test-as-rules.test.ts` (64: rows, each enforcement point present, every `app/api` route reading the testing state classified, attribution list valid, safe methods), `tests/api/access-requests-route.test.ts` (9: sends only for a refused area, page named only for that area, grants nothing, refused while testing, admins/admin-holders/cleaners refused, held area 409, unknown area 400, cross-site 403, policy read failure 503, 401), `tests/components/test-as-and-access-denied.test.tsx` (13: grouping/folding, search and chips, view only start, tier A full confirm and failure kept, relock redirect, table rows from rules, banner words/end time/phone Stop 44px, failed stop, office page one h1 + Ask flow + failure kept, unknown state, portal look), `tests/db/test-as-access-notices.test.ts` (5, fresh `testas_fresh` on 55439: inbox-only to both administrators and not the requester, policy unchanged, racing clicks → one request, per-area limit, full-access note once with who/when only, none after view only, sweep once). ## Cleaner performance figures and record-page tabs (2026-11-18)

Scope: `docs/SYSTEM.md` › Workforce › "Performance figure definitions (2026-11-18)" and the Change Log line. Two owner reports from production. No schema change; live session/role guards unchanged; no toured page changed (neither `/v2/admin/workforce/performance/[id]` nor `/v2/admin/jobs/[id]` has a tour; `tests/lib/tutorials-up-to-date.test.ts` passes unchanged).

**Bug 1, performance page figures.** Cause, per figure (old → new in SYSTEM.md): scheduled start was the Sydney start time written as UTC (`setUTCHours`), 10–11 h after the real start, so every clock-in looked hours early ("498 min early") and every GPS arrival "on time" (100%); punctuality averaged every time-log segment; "Photos and forms" divided by submissions made, so jobs with no form disappeared from the denominator; "Disputes" counted every IssueTicket of any type on any job ever assigned (removed and cancelled included) over assignments created in the window; attendance and no-shows divided by assignments created in the window (future, cancelled and removed included), and a no-show was a declined offer; quality averaged QA form submissions minus a hidden rework penalty, unlike the Quality hub. Fixed at the source: new pure `lib/workforce/performance-figures.ts`, `getPerformanceMetrics` rewritten to read the cleaner's own active jobs in the Sydney-day window plus offers; one definition shared with People › Cleaners, person KPIs and classic screens; Insights cleaner rows now use each cleaner's own first clock-in. Minimum sample 3 (existing `MIN_BASIS_ROW`), denominators on every row and tile, "How this is worked out" per figure. Score words and limits unchanged. A failed jobs/offers read now fails the window instead of returning zeros (the person page and classic account page catch it and hide the KPIs).

**Bug 2, job page tabs.** Reproduced in Chromium at 1280 (mouse) and 390 (touch): after Photos (or any tab), Timeline could never be chosen again — the URL stayed `?view=photos`. Not the `hidden` attribute issue and not an overlay: view memory (`components/shared/view-memory-provider.tsx`, `lib/navigation/view-memory.ts`) treated the bare Timeline link as a return to the page and appended the remembered `?view=photos`; on a full navigation the `pagehide` save re-recorded the view being left. Same defect on every record page whose default tab is the bare URL (client page "Overview"). Fix: a bare link (or router push) to the page already open is the viewer choosing its default view (`rememberChosenView`, `settleChosenView`); links that carry a query, other pages and other origins keep the return-to-last-view behaviour.

**Tests.** New `tests/lib/workforce-performance-figures.test.ts` (17: fixture with no-form jobs, no clock-in, DST-straddling Sydney clock-ins, one real dispute, team job, cancelled/skipped, offers; owner regression), `tests/db/workforce-performance.test.ts` (2, real PostgreSQL on 55439: removed assignment, OPS/DAMAGE/SLA/lost-property cases, cancelled dispute and pay adjustments ignored, teammate clock-in not counted, 30/90-day windows; fails against the previous loader), `tests/lib/insights-cleaner-on-time.test.ts` (2), `tests/components/view-memory-tab-switching.test.tsx` (3: job and client tab strips back and forth; fails without the fix), 3 new cases in `tests/lib/view-memory.test.ts`, 1 new case in `tests/components/workforce-redesign.test.tsx`; `tests/lib/performance.test.ts` rewritten for the new loader (7).

**Not verified.** Production build and full suite not run (not requested). Maintenance request, damage report, onboarding workspace, shopping run, pay run, cleaner invoice and inspection record pages were checked by reading their switchers (client-state tabs or `#section` chips, no bare-URL tab links), not in a browser, for lack of fixture records. Production data not inspected; the owner's screenshot pattern was reproduced on a fixture. Deployment not verified.

## Guest timing tags and the cleaner's timing acknowledgement (2026-11-17)

Scope: `docs/SYSTEM.md` › B3 "Guest timing tags on office screens" and "Was the timing acknowledged?", B5 start briefing ("Timing confirmation that follows the time"), and the Change Log line. Owner request: early check-in / late checkout not showing clearly as tags in admin; confirm the cleaner portal shows them and that cleaners acknowledge them. No schema change, no migration, no `lib/scheduling-rules/**` change. Continued from an unreviewed checkpoint (`8cca5fb3`), which was reviewed and completed.

**Admin.** One derivation (`resolveTimingBadges`, attached server-side by each loader) and one tag component (`components/v2/ui/timing-tags.tsx`) on Jobs rows, phone cards and board cards (replacing "EARLY 13:00" / "LATE 11:00"), the job page's "Guest timing" section under the facts, the job preview (also opened from the calendar), calendar chips (now wrapping with the shared component instead of being cut to "Late ch…"), calendar agenda and phone agenda, and Command (upcoming list; "Guest timing today" plus a table column under Today by cleaner). Unreadable stored times are stated as "… · time could not be read". The job page states each cleaner's acknowledgement from the stored record: "acknowledged the current timing · <time>", "not acknowledged yet", "not acknowledged for the current timing … earlier version", or "could not be read".

**Cleaner.** Today offer cards now show the timing before Accept (jobs list, calendar, route and job page already did, confirmed in the browser). (a) The start gate already refused a first start without every briefing item, timing first; confirmed against the running app (409 `START_BRIEFING_REQUIRED`, reason MISSING, two TIMING_RULE items) and pinned by tests. (b) Acks were already content-hashed; added: the start request must echo the fingerprint of the list shown when a timing rule exists, each ack carries a timing-only fingerprint, and a cleaner already working is asked in the app to read a changed time again (new `POST /api/cleaner/jobs/[id]/briefing-ack`, guarded write, refused while testing as the cleaner). (c) The office change reaches assigned cleaners through the existing job-updated delivery in `PATCH /api/admin/jobs/[id]`; no new email, SMS or push was added. Observed on the fixture: a late-checkout change also moves the start time, so the delivery used the general job-update kind with "Immediate attention" (a PUSH row recorded SENT and an EMAIL row FAILED for want of a provider), not the narrower `timing_rule_changed` kind.

**Tours.** Jobs v3 (step "timing" on the table), Command v2 ("guestTiming"), cleaner Today v2 ("offerTiming"); release note `2026.11.17-guest-timing`; fingerprints regenerated with the script. ## Slow-connection video uploads (2026-11-17)

Scope: `docs/SYSTEM.md` › B6 "Slow-connection video uploads (2026-11-17)" and the Change Log line; follow-up section in `docs/qa/cleaner-upload-reliability-2026-10-28.md`. Owner report: large videos keep failing for some cleaners while the same video works for others. No schema change, no migration, no route or permission change, no `lib/scheduling-rules/**`, `mobile/`, `mobile-app/` or `sneek-nextgen/` change. **Cause inferred from code only**; no production logs, failure rows or the affected phone were available.

**Confirmed in code before the change.** Fixed 120 s total deadline per 5 MB part with three parts sharing the uplink (needs about 1 Mbps upload); any thrown direct PUT, including that timeout, switched the rest of the upload to the application proxy; three attempts with 0.5/1 s backoff; no byte progress within a part; signed URLs expire after an hour with only the proxy as recovery. Also found: storage's idle-socket HTTP 400 `RequestTimeout` was treated as permanent; compression falls back to the (often much larger) original where WebCodecs or OPFS writing is missing; the v2 capture logged no failures at all; Node's default 300 s request timeout bounds one proxied part (not changed).

**Changed.** New `lib/uploads/part-transport.ts` (XHR with upload progress, 60 s stall window, twice that after the last byte, re-armed while the tab is hidden, 15-minute ceiling; fetch fallback bounded by the ceiling). `lib/uploads/multipart-client.ts`: probe part alone, then 3 / 2 / 1 parts at ≥256 KB/s / ≥96 KB/s / slower, 1 after any stall; 5 attempts, 1/2/4/8 s backoff; storage 400 `RequestTimeout`/`IncompleteBody` retried; offline waits up to 5 minutes for `online` without spending attempts; stalls retried on the same route; proxy only for an immediate zero-byte refusal while online, a missing ETag, or a 403 that a URL refresh through `resume-multipart` (same key and upload id only) cannot fix; live byte progress that never moves backwards or double counts; connection advice and failure statistics callbacks (no URLs or keys). `components/v2/cleaner/media-capture.tsx`: "Connection" advice lines (slow, offline, and a failed line that replaces "still uploading"); a finally failed, not cancelled, video posts to the existing log-failure endpoint with scalar context (kbps, parts, stalled/refused/network/offline/setup/receipt, direct/proxy, concurrency, stalls, last status, effective connection type, online, encoding). `lib/uploads/failure-context.ts` summarises that context on `/admin/system/uploads` and System health › Uploads. The classic dropzone gets the transport change unchanged otherwise. Earlier fixes kept: 30 s control deadlines, resumed allocations never aborted, confirmed parts never re-sent, completion receipt checks, cancel without fallback, one-video lane.

**Tests.** `tests/lib/part-transport.test.ts` (new, 12), `tests/lib/multipart-client.test.ts` (40 existing, 20 new XHR cases: slow-but-moving part past 120 s, stall retried on the same route, CORS-style refusal to proxy, drop after bytes retried, storage idle 400 retried, 403 refresh and refresh mismatch to proxy, offline wait, offline failures not counted, bounded offline give-up, monotonic progress across a retry, concurrency 1 on slow / 3 on fast / thresholds / 1 after stall, cancel mid-part, resume skipping confirmed parts, failure statistics, refusal statistics, slow advice once), `tests/lib/upload-pipeline.test.ts` (7 new: telemetry content for stalled, unsupported-compression and compressed videos, no log on cancel or success or photo failure, connection advice wording, failed line), `tests/lib/upload-request.test.ts` (1 new: 30 s control default with a longer part deadline), `tests/lib/upload-failure-context.test.ts` (new, 2). Changed existing assertions: "bounds a stalled transfer and falls back" asserted the old stall-to-proxy switch that this change removes; it now asserts the opposite (ceiling, retried direct, proxy never called). Two existing cases now run on fake timers because of the longer backoff; the receipt-read timeout case advances five ceilings; the resumed-parts case additionally asserts five attempts on the failing part. Existing fetch-path cases stub XHR away so they keep testing the fallback.

**Inspected (Chromium headless, `next dev` on 3214 against `sneek_ui`, cleaner on `demo-job-001`, Arrival walkthrough video "Choose video").** Playwright route interception stood in for presign, the storage PUTs, complete, abort and log-failure; a 12 MB fake MP4 was chosen. Seen: compression refused (original used, existing advice), one allocation, part 1 sent alone, storage never answered, "Connection: Your connection is slow. Keep this page open; the video is still uploading." appeared after 60 s; four more attempts on the same direct URL about 61/62/64/68 s apart (stall plus 1/2/4/8 s), zero proxy requests, no abort request; then "1 file did not upload. Retry the saved upload below…", the Connection line replaced by the failed line, Retry upload button focusable, and one log-failure POST with `S3_TIMEOUT`, `encoding: original_unsupported`, `partsDone 0/3`, `stalls 5`, `route direct`, `effectiveType 4g`, `speedKbps null` and no URL or signature. The advice block at 390 and 1280, light and dark: no page or line overflow, screenshots inspected. **Could not be shown in the browser:** real upload progress or a slow-but-moving transfer (interception gives no upload progress events, so the activity bar stayed at 0% and the stall fired after 60 s rather than after the body); adaptive concurrency above 1; URL refresh; offline waits; these rely on the unit tests with fake timers. No real storage, real phone or slow cellular link was used. The capture's durable record stayed only in the closed browser context; nothing was written to the fixture database by the interception run.

**Tours.** No tour target changed; the advice uses the existing notice; `tests/lib/tutorials-up-to-date.test.ts` passed without a fingerprint update, so no tour or `--no-visible-change` entry was made.

**Run.** `npx tsc --noEmit -p .` clean. Full non-DB suite `npx vitest run --exclude 'tests/db/**'`: 1122 files passed, 1 skipped (11982 tests passed, 1 skipped). Production build passed (842/842 pages, exit 0; closed database URL, expected unavailable-database and static-render warnings). No DB suites (no database code touched). No deployment; whether phones receive the new bundle (PWA cache) is unverified.

Owner: "Maintenance tasks: admin should be able to ask the client to do it, remind it, assign to the client so he can assign his own maintenance person." - **Decisions on the owner's behalf (pending confirmation):** hand-over refused while our visit worker or roster is active (removed first, history kept; nothing removed implicitly) and while our price is pending or approved; a manual reminder needs 4 hours since the last one that reached a transport; client "done" resolves the item (CP-7 resolves a linked damage case); decline / could-not-do / take-back return it to the office and withdraw only a visit that came from the client's plan; hand-over and reminder default to portal inbox + email, chosen per send; the office's progress notice is inbox only; recipients of client messages are the client's CLIENT logins (assistants act but do not receive these messages).

- **Browser (Chromium, dev server on `sneek_ui`):** full flow at 1280 light: admin hands over with a note (keyboard Enter on the disclosure; note kept after folding) → card "With the client" with inbox "In their portal inbox" and email "Failed: the email was not accepted (no provider set up, or it refused)" — **no email provider is configured locally, so no email was actually delivered** → client sees "For you to arrange", accepts, empty-name save shows the error, enters tradesperson + 20 Nov 09:00–10:30 (draft kept after folding) → admin sees the answer, tradesperson and "Shown to the cleaner on that day's job"; the list filter "With the client" works → the cleaner's job JOB-001000 (20 Nov) shows "Maintenance visit — Walk: dripping kitchen tap … Contractor: Sam Sparkes · 0400 111 222 · Arranged by the client" in Read first at 390 → admin reminds (outcome shown; second reminder disabled with "next one can be sent from …", API 409) → client marks done → admin sees "Client marked it done" with the note. Second item: client hands back with a reason → admin "Client declined", list row "Client declined to arrange it". Third item: admin takes it back with a reason. Operations manager GET 200; a cleaner gets 403 on both routes. At 390 light/dark and 1280 dark: admin item card, "Ask the client again" disclosure, admin list and the client section were screenshotted; no horizontal overflow; one h1 and one main on both pages.

Deployment not done.

## Pay requests: link to jobs, combined on invoices, edit, mark paid, full details (2026-11-18)

Owner request (verbatim in `docs/SYSTEM.md` › D3 "Pay requests: linked to jobs, combined on invoices, edited, marked paid, shown in full"). Migration `20261118030000_pay_request_links_and_settlement` (additive). No `lib/scheduling-rules/**`, `mobile/`, `mobile-app/` or `sneek-nextgen/` change.

- **Already true before:** every cleaner path linked a request raised from a job (the job-page "approval request" route left property/scope/title empty — fixed); an approved job-linked extra already counted once in the job pay summary, the payroll summary (separate signed row) and the cleaner invoice (folded). Gaps fixed: money-back extras folded into the taxable job line; no per-extra details on any line; re-linking checked only that the job existed; nothing reached the client invoice; no paid-outside state; no full view after a decision; the job page money rail left approved extras out of cleaner pay and margin.
- **Decisions made for the owner (pending confirmation):** bill-to-client default ON (`invoicing.payRequestsBillClientByDefault`) for job-linked SERVICE requests whose property has a client, OFF for parking/reimbursements/unlinked; only requests approved after this change; quick and bulk approve refuse rows the default would bill so the panel's "This will add $X…" statement is seen; client amount defaults to the office's figure, else the amount the client approved via send-to-client, else the approved amount; awaiting/countered/declined/expired client consent is never billed; an extra whose job is on a non-draft invoice lands on the next invoice as its own line (never altering the issued one); a fully credited invoice does not release its extras (conservative); mark paid is ADMIN-only with own PIN (`pay_requests.mark_paid`, like payout processing), positive approved amounts only, undo needs a reason, no email/push; a reduction after approval (lower, decline, reopen) needs a reason the cleaner reads; linking requires the payee actively assigned to an accepted job at the same property; the folded client description is one line (" — Base service $X; Extra: … $Y") because the editor's description field is single-line; client lines show the client wording or title and hours, never the cleaner's rate, pay or note.
- **Migration verified:** `prisma migrate deploy` on a fresh disposable database (165 migrations), on an already-migrated disposable database and on `sneek_ui`; repeated: "No pending migrations to apply"; `prisma migrate diff --from-url … --to-schema-datamodel prisma/schema.prisma --exit-code` exit 0 on each.

## Stock used table question (2026-11-20)

Owner request (verbatim in `docs/SYSTEM.md` › "Stock used table question (2026-11-20)"). No schema change, no migration. No `lib/scheduling-rules/**`, `mobile/`, `mobile-app/` or `sneek-nextgen/` change.

- **Cause of the error:** the seed template's per-section stock tables (`type: "inventory"`, `items: [SKU…]`) had no case in the v2 cleaner renderer (rendered as a plain text box labelled "Items used") and, since the forms Checks were added (`lib/forms/template-checks.ts`, commit `ae7e4c1c`), the builder showed them as "Unknown (inventory)" with the error "has a question type no form can show (inventory)" — `LEGACY_TYPES` there held only `upload`/`textarea`. Reproduced on `origin/main` in Chromium before the change (builder Checks: two such errors plus the unrelated `laundry_confirm` one; cleaner Kitchen section: a single `input[type=text]`).
- **Built:** `inventory` registered as "Stock used"; Show (all / kitchen / bathroom / laundry / other from existing location and category) and "Include items with none on hand"; legacy `items`/`location` still read; shared per-item answer `stockTableUsage` (unanswered vs 0); one required/whole-number rule for phone and server; server-read cleaner-safe stock list with an `unavailable` state; table replaces the block for its items; merged usage through the existing `deductJobStockOnce`; `__stockTable` record for office views; client report only under the theme's Supplies section; classic cleaner page uses the same rows and key; wrap-up tally counts table usage.
- **Decisions made for the owner (pending confirmation):** overlapping tables share one answer per item (Checks warn, never refuse) rather than refusing overlaps, so "Kitchen + Bathroom + All items" works; a table replaces the "Stock & consumables used" block for the items it shows (warning until the block is set to show none); "Include items with none on hand" defaults ON (a stale 0 must not hide an item that was used); Laundry and Other come from the item category because the location list has no laundry value; legacy section-title guessing is not carried over; counting stays with the Stock count question; the office answer editor keeps the type locked; a failed stock read no longer fails the whole job form.

Builder at 390: page loads, no horizontal overflow (fields not added at that width). Cleaner on `demo-job-001` at 390 and 1280, light and dark: kitchen and bathroom tables list the property's items with unit and on-hand, "Not answered yet" until answered; −/+ and typing, "−" on an unanswered row records "None used", ArrowUp on the box, "-1" refused in words; values kept across a reload; no horizontal overflow at 390, including an item temporarily renamed to a 110-character name (wraps; restored); the Stock & consumables block (Extras › Stock) lists only items no table shows. ## Property devices: Ring, Minut and charge tasks on the next clean (2026-11-20)

- **Start:** branch `sneek/optimistic-curie-fwry7f` restarted from main `dd9725c5`, clean. Read AGENTS.md, SYSTEM.md (property, JobTasks, notifications, integrations, scheduler, forms device status) and this record; mapped the JobTask service, the submit gate, carry-forward, the lost and found task hook, Read first, the pending property tasks and their attach hooks, the cancel path, the worker and System health registry, the notification office-send pipeline and catalog, the integrations credentials, the Xero token rotation, the settings catalog and the ops packs.
- **Built:** migration `20261120120000_property_devices` (additive). Register, readings, threshold rule (25% / 48 h stale / 72 h cool-down / 6 h poll / notice after 3 failures, all settings), one task per raise under the device row lock through the JobTask service, enforced on submit (photo when done, reason when not, never not applicable), carry-forward with the device kept, cancel → next clean, waiting task attached by the existing hooks and hourly; Ring custom read-only and Minut official connectors behind one allow-list; settings screen, property Devices panel, Properties attention list and nav count; inbox notices for cleaners and for a failing connection; reminder emails and the daily briefing list the charge line. Tutorial `cleaner.job.practice` v2 (checklist step), release note, fingerprints refreshed with the script.
- **Owner-facing decisions (defaults, pending owner confirmation):** CLEANER readings are evidence and do not close a low-battery condition (the brief named provider or office readings); office may close with a note; OPS_MANAGER via the Properties pack (manage to add devices and readings), thresholds ADMIN only; the cleaner and office notices are inbox only; only Ring cameras and doorbells are imported (not chimes, base stations, bridges); an offline Ring camera's battery figure is not recorded; a Minut device with no report time records no reading; locations are suggested by name/address but linked only by the office; the task carries "Could not do" quick reasons.
- **Not changed / left alone:** `lib/scheduling-rules/**` (byte-identical), jobs are never created or moved; the form `device_status` question records yes/no without a percent or a device, so it is not converted into readings; the single-job reminder wording; no client surface.
- **References:** Ring shapes from github.com/dgreif/ring (`packages/ring-client-api/rest-client.ts`, `api.ts`, `ring-types.ts`), Minut token from developer.minut.com/authentication/user, homes/devices fields from github.com/fredrike/pypoint (`const.py`, `__init__.py`); Minut's own reference (api.minut.com/latest/docs) could not be read here. **Neither Ring nor Minut was contacted**; clients are verified against recorded-shape fixtures only.
- **Migration:** `prisma migrate deploy` on a fresh disposable database and on an existing migrated copy, and on fixture `sneek_ui`; repeated (no pending); `migrate diff --exit-code` 0 on all three.
- **Checks:** TypeScript passed. New tests 42 (30 unit + 12 PostgreSQL). Full non-DB suite first run: 27 failures in 7 files, all from db mocks lacking the new reads (attention count, reminder charge lines); mocks extended and the reminder lookup made failure-tolerant (logged); the 7 files then passed. Full non-DB suite re-run: **1148 files passed, 1 skipped; 12259 tests passed, 1 skipped**. DB suites on a fresh database: **67 files passed, 1 skipped; 438 tests passed, 1 skipped**. Production build passed (843 pages, exit 0).
- **Browser (Chromium, real dev server on `sneek_ui`):** admin settings › Ring and Minut devices at 1280 light, 1280 dark and 390 dark: one h1/main, no horizontal overflow, the Ring warning, both providers "Not connected", Minut's missing id and secret named. Property › Care and planning › Devices at 1280 light: added "Front door" by hand, entered 18% → "a charge task was put on the next clean", Low battery, Needs charging, link to the clean (today's offered job). Cleaner at 390 light and 1280 dark on that offered job: High priority in Read first. To see the Clean stage the task was temporarily moved to the fixture's in-progress `demo-job-001` (restored by deletion below): at 390 dark and 1280 light the card shows High priority, no "no photo" excuse, the device photo label and the battery field (digits only); "Important requests" dialog lists it; Wrap up shows the task blocker while undecided, while Done has no photo, and while Could not do has no reason; choosing the quick reason (keyboard Enter) clears it. Admin at 390 dark: Properties › Devices needing attention opened by keyboard, the row and reason; nav Properties count 1; entered 80% by keyboard → "The charge is closed", OK, task cancelled in the database; a 150% entry was refused with the entry kept. The cleaner's inbox notice was queued and delivered to the inbox row (pipeline, inbox only).
- **Not inspected:** a photo upload and a full job submission in the browser (S3 and the rest of the form; the done-with-photo path and the submit gate are covered by the PostgreSQL test through `applyCleanerJobTaskUpdates` and by the route check), the connected states and location linking in a browser (no provider), operations-manager screens in a browser (pack rules unit-tested), Minut/Ring real services, email/SMS/push delivery, phones, deployment.
- **Fixture rows:** the walk's device, its two readings, the charge task and its events, the cleaner's inbox notification, intent and attempt, the dispatch receipt and the device audit row were deleted. A `UserSignIn` cleanup by time window deleted 52 rows, more than the walk's ~16 sign-ins: sign-in history rows from earlier the same day on the fixture were removed too and cannot be restored. Nothing else from the walk remains.

## Lost and found: stay details in guest messages and on the guest page (2026-11-20)

Owner request (verbatim): "For guests always mention the stay date, location address, name, etc to easily identify the stay. This is for lost and found I mean". No schema change, no scheduling rule changed.

- **Changed:** guest page rows Guest, Stay, Property, Address; the Message guest draft, the approve and decline drafts (office and client) and the automatic acknowledgement carry a "Your stay" block (guest, stay dates, property with address). Acknowledgement placeholder `{{stayDetails}}`; a saved template without it still gets the block after its first paragraph. Texts over 459 characters use a one-line form.
- **Reverses an earlier choice:** the guest page used to withhold the property address; it is shown now on the owner's instruction. Description, storage place, value, cleaner, guest contact and access codes stay hidden (DB test still asserts this).
- **Limits:** stay dates come only from the booking linked to the item. An item with no linked booking shows no stay dates (not guessed); the office can type them into the draft before sending. The longer Message guest draft can pass the 459-character text limit, in which case the dialog already asks the office to shorten it or send by email.

## Forms: the seed's laundry question renders as yes/no (2026-11-20)

Owner: "Ok fix the laundry confirm thing". The seed's `laundry_confirm` question type had no renderer (builder error "a question type no form can show"; a text box for cleaners, so the "if YES" follow-ups never showed). It is now aliased to `yesno` in `lib/forms/normalize-schema.ts` (also `lint-template.ts`, `template-checks.ts`). No schema change, no stored template rewritten. Answers already saved as text stay as they were stored.


## Personal notification switches: the client who switched notifications off, and two-way switches for every portal (2026-11-21)

Owner request (verbatim in `docs/SYSTEM.md` › "Personal notification switches: one two-way model for every portal (2026-11-21)"). Migration `20261121090000_personal_notification_switches` (additive). No `lib/scheduling-rules/**`, `mobile/`, `mobile-app/` or `sneek-nextgen/` change.

**Every automatic message that can reach a client (before → after).** "Checked" = what the sender looked at before this change. "Master off" = a client with Settings › Enable notifications off.

| Message | Sender | Transports | Checked before | Master off: sent before? | After |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| On the way / arrived / started / finished (`client.job_updates`) | `lib/notifications/client-job-notifications.ts` (cleaner driving and status routes) | inbox, phone push, email, text | client master, 3 events, preferred channel, email kind | No | No (also per type/transport) |
| Booked, reminder, cleaner assigned, rescheduled, cancelled/skipped, complete, report ready, issue raised, re-clean (`client.lifecycle_email`, auto) | `lib/notifications/lifecycle.ts` (job create, reminders, assign, skip, edit, QA rework, submit) | email | client master (auto mode), email kind | No | No |
| Post-job automation rules: report and review emails and texts (`client.post_job`) | `lib/notifications/client-automation.ts` (worker after submit) | email, text | email kind, audience, login email prefs | **Yes** | No |
| Follow-up sequence 1, 3, 14 days (`client.post_job`) | `lib/ops/follow-up-sequences.ts` (worker) | email | email kind `follow_up` | **Yes** | No |
| Case created/updated/followed up, client-visible (`office.cases`) | `lib/cases/notifications.ts`, `lib/cases/follow-up.ts` | inbox, phone push, email (also to the client's own address) | login topic `cases`, email kind/audience | **Yes** | No |
| Payment receipt (`client.documents`, kind `auto_invoice`) | `lib/billing/client-payment-receipt.ts` (record payment, Xero pull) | email | email kind | **Yes** | No |
| Shopping run completed (`stock.shopping_and_orders`) | `lib/inventory/client-shopping-notifications.ts` | email | login topic `shopping` email | **Yes** | No |
| Job-task request approved/declined, auto-approved (to the requesting client) | `lib/job-tasks/service.ts` via `deliverNotificationToRecipients` | inbox, phone push, email, text | login topic `jobs`/`approvals` | **Yes** | No |
| Guest asked for a lost item back (`lostfound.client_return_request`) | `lib/lost-found/return-notices.ts` (pipeline inbox) | inbox | login topic | **Yes** (inbox) | No |
| Phone push of any client inbox row | `lib/notifications/mobile-push.ts` | phone push | login topic `web` | **Yes** for the rows above | No |
| Any future automatic sender | — | any | — | (would have) | No: chokepoints |
| Delay notice / lost and found client notice / damage released / repair handed over or reminder | `office-actions.ts`, `lost-found/client-notice.ts`, `damage/release-notice.ts`, `maintenance/client-handling-notices.ts` | inbox, push, email, text | client master: refused with a reason | No (refused) | Unchanged: still refused |
| Quote send, quote extras, report share, invoice send (single/bulk), document re-send, client hub lifecycle send, bulk client update, composer / portal replies, booking answer, approval request, pay adjustment to client, QA invite, onboarding and amenities links | send buttons (listed in `tests/lib/personal-switch-coverage.test.ts`) | email, text, inbox | none (human decision) | Yes (by design) | Yes, marked `officeDecision` |
| Sign-in codes, password reset, two-step, invitations, backup code used, tested as | auth modules | email, inbox | none (`critical`) | Yes | Yes (always sent) |
| Email/SMS campaigns | `lib/marketing/*` | email, text | campaign unsubscribe, marketing consent | Yes | Unchanged (own consent; shown as such) |
| Finance event templates (`money.finance_events`) | `lib/notifications/engine.ts` | email, text | per-event switches | catalog: not sent today | Would be stopped by the chokepoints |

**Fix:** `checkPersonalSend` in `email.ts` (per address), `sms.ts`, `web-push.ts`, `mobile-push.ts` (before the sending safeguards) and `applyPersonalInboxSwitches` in the Prisma middleware for every inbox row. Recipient found by account or by address (logins, the client's own email/phone, client logins' account, delivery lists). Skips: email `personalSkip` / row `deliveryStatus SKIPPED` → Delivery log "Skipped"; inbox row FAILED with "Not sent: this client has switched these notifications off.", hidden from the inbox, never pushed; pipeline SKIPPED `recipient_switched_off`. Fail rule: allowed and logged when switches cannot be read (as the existing email personal layer).

- **Decisions made for the owner (pending confirmation):**
  1. Client master off stops every automatic message, including payment receipts, automatic lifecycle emails and case updates (the office can still send any document by hand, and sees the switch on the client page).
  2. Messages a person sends with a send button still go (marked `officeDecision`); the four office actions that already refused a client with notifications off keep refusing (no override added).
  3. Campaigns are not governed by these switches (their own marketing consent and one-click unsubscribe); shown on the screen as such.
  4. Always sent: sign-in, password reset, two-step, invitations, backup code used, tested-as notice, the safeguards' alert to administrators; operational types marked IGNORES (e.g. shift cancelled, clock changes) and office-directed types (unless "respects preferences" is on for the type) are shown as "Always sent" / "Sent only when someone in the office chooses" and cannot be switched off by the person.
  5. ADMIN alone edits other people's switches; OPS_MANAGER reads (Messages & notifications pack). Three older routes became ADMIN-only for writes: client preferences PUT, a person's email preferences PUT, bulk email preferences.
  6. A client login edits the client account's switches (they apply to all logins and to the client's own address/phone); a login's own switches are editable by an administrator on the person page (and by staff on their own pages).
  7. Every write refused while testing as someone else (view-only and full), including the older client route.
  8. Client job updates: "How cleaning updates reach you" keeps EMAIL / SMS / BOTH; switching both off is stored as two type switches.
  9. Stopped inbox rows are kept as "not sent" records (status FAILED) rather than not written.
- **Not changed:** classic (non-v2) pages keep their older controls (they write through the same path where they used the client route); topic (category) switches keep their meaning and enforcement points; no per-type staff switch for a message whose type the producer does not name (only All / per transport then apply — documented on SYSTEM.md).
- **Migration verified:** fresh disposable database `sneek_np_fresh` (all migrations), repeated "No pending migrations to apply", `migrate diff --exit-code` 0; existing database `sneek_np_existing` migrated to the previous head, a client stored with notifications off / en route off / SMS, then the new migration applied: values unchanged, new columns empty, revision 0; repeated, diff 0; fixture `sneek_ui` applied, repeated, diff 0.
- **Tests:** new `tests/lib/personal-switches-decide.test.ts` (16), `tests/lib/personal-switches-enforce.test.ts` (10), `tests/lib/personal-switch-coverage.test.ts` (5), `tests/api/notification-switches-routes.test.ts` (6), `tests/components/personal-switches-panel.test.tsx` (6), `tests/db/personal-notification-switches.test.ts` (7, PostgreSQL). Updated expectations in `notification-history`, `notification-inbox-state` (inbox query hides skipped rows), `laundry-supplier-chase` (SMS carries the office-decision marker); `test-as-rules` gained the three self-service switch routes. Full non-DB suite first run: 22 failures in 6 files (all from this change: expectations above, two older routes failing a saved change when the new stamp/audit could not be written — now logged, never a failed save); those 6 files then passed. DB suites on a fresh disposable database (`--no-file-parallelism`, dropped afterwards): **68 files passed, 1 skipped; 445 tests passed, 1 skipped**. TypeScript passed. Production build passed (`next build` 279 s).
- **Browser (Chromium headless, dev server on 3221 against `sneek_ui`, service workers blocked, a throwaway local NEXTAUTH_SECRET because the environment has none):** client Settings, cleaner Settings and admin client page › Notifications at 390 and 1280, light and dark: one h1, one main, no sideways scroll (all 12). Flow: client switched "Case created…: Email" off with the keyboard (Space) and "Enable notifications" off → admin (1280) saw master off with "Changed by Demo Client, 22 Nov 2026, 11:25 am", "Only what is switched off" listed the case row (not job updates), switched both back on → Notifications › Preferences list said nobody has anything off → client (390 dark) saw master on with "Changed by Admin User …".
- **Not inspected in a browser:** laundry, maintenance, QA and office profile panels (built and typed; same component), the person page Notifications tab, an operations manager's read-only view and testing-as (route and component tests only), the stale-write message (component test only), the off-list with entries (DB test only), real email/SMS/push delivery (providers stubbed), real phones, deployment.
- **Fixture rows:** the walk's 8 audit rows and the `ClientNotificationPreference` row it created for `demo-client-001` were deleted by id (none existed before). Left: sign-in side effects of the walk's logins (not deleted; no time-window deletes).

## One-off jobs, cleaner invoice amendments and the Xero account for cleaner bills (2026-11-23)

Owner requests (verbatim in `docs/SYSTEM.md` › "One-off jobs reach their client…", "Office amendments of submitted cleaner invoices", "Xero account for cleaner invoices"). Migration `20261123090000_cleaner_invoice_amendments` (additive). No `lib/scheduling-rules/**`, `mobile/`, `mobile-app/` or `sneek-nextgen/` change.

**One-off jobs: cause, reproduced on `sneek_ui`.** A one-off job created through `POST /api/admin/jobs` for Harbour View Stays and completed was listed on the client page (Properties and Jobs tabs), in the admin Jobs list, in the client portal's jobs and properties, and in New invoice's preview (priced from the price list). No code filtered one-off jobs out of job lists, the portal or the generator. The failing case: a one-off made without choosing a client (the New job default "No linked client") is filed under the catch-all client "Unassigned / One-off Jobs", and nothing could move it (the job PATCH deletes `clientId`; property updates omit `clientId`). Fix: Attach to client (one-off sites under the catch-all only, refused while a job is on an invoice), the generator refuses the catch-all client with the way forward, New job warns, converted one-off quotes carry their agreed subtotal, the client page groups One-off sites. Kept exclusions and why are listed in SYSTEM.md.

**Cleaner invoice amendments.** Who may amend by state, the revision history, the stale and idempotency rules, the Xero flag and the payee notice are in SYSTEM.md. Decisions made for the owner (pending confirmation):
1. OPS_MANAGER amends only SUBMITTED invoices; after approval (APPROVED, XERO_PUSHED) only ADMIN, because the amendment changes approved money and only an administrator approves. An admin's amendment keeps an approved invoice approved.
2. Paid invoices (or any recorded payment), payment claims, pushes in flight, delivery review, sent-back and reversed invoices are refused with their reason; paid ones point to a pay adjustment.
3. The Xero client cannot update a bill, so nothing is sent to Xero: the invoice is flagged "Needs updating in Xero" until someone records "I've updated the bill in Xero" with a note.
4. Removing a line does not hand its job back to the payee (send it back for changes for that).
5. The payee's notice respects their personal switches (it is not an office-decision exemption); the inbox row has no phone or browser push; the email links to the amended PDF rather than attaching it (the pipeline carries no attachments).
6. An amended invoice cannot be hard-deleted (reverse it instead) so its history stays.
7. Amending is refused while testing as someone else.

**Xero account for cleaner bills.** No account had ever been stored for cleaner bills (every bill went to 400), so pushes are now refused until an administrator chooses the cleaning work account in Settings › Money › Xero › Cleaner bills. Other hard-coded `"200"` fallbacks (client invoices, credit notes, Update in Xero, CSV export, shopping mapping, the editor display, the stored default) are reported in SYSTEM.md and not changed.

- **Migration verified:** fresh disposable database `sneek_t_fresh`: 169 migrations applied, repeated "No pending migrations to apply", `migrate diff --exit-code` 0 ("No difference detected"). Existing database `sneek_t_oneoff` (migrated to the previous head earlier in the session, then the new migration applied): applied, repeated "No pending", diff 0. Fixture `sneek_ui` (two cleaner invoice rows already present, created for the walk): applied, repeated "No pending", diff 0; the rows' totals unchanged and `amendmentCount` 0. Both disposable databases dropped.
- **Tests:** new `tests/lib/one-off-jobs.test.ts` (8), `tests/db/one-off-jobs-client.test.ts` (4), `tests/lib/cleaner-bill-accounts.test.ts` (5), `tests/components/xero-cleaner-bill-accounts.test.tsx` (3), `tests/lib/cleaner-invoice-amend-rules.test.ts` (7), `tests/components/amend-invoice-panel.test.tsx` (4), `tests/api/cleaner-invoice-amend-route.test.ts` (3), `tests/db/cleaner-invoice-amendments.test.ts` (6, real office pipeline, email provider stubbed); extended `tests/api/cleaner-invoice-export-guards.test.ts` (+2: refusal before any reservation, per-line accounts) and `tests/lib/xero-idempotency-transport.test.ts` (+1: the account sent, a line without one never reaches Xero). Full non-DB suite first run: 1 failure (`tests/lib/email-html-scan.test.ts`: the amendment notice built HTML fragments in code; moved into wording parts), then **1160 files passed, 1 skipped; 12345 tests passed, 1 skipped**. DB suites on a fresh disposable database (`--no-file-parallelism`): **70 files passed, 1 skipped; 455 tests passed, 1 skipped**. TypeScript passed. Production build passed (`next build`, 843 pages; a first attempt ran the type check out of memory at the script's default 4096 MB heap and was re-run with `NEXT_BUILD_MEMORY_MB=7168`).
- **Browser (Chromium 1243 headless, dev server on 3221 against `sneek_ui`, service workers blocked, a throwaway NEXTAUTH_SECRET):** at 390 and 1280, light and dark, one h1, one main and no sideways scroll on: Settings › Xero; the catch-all client's Properties tab; the unlinked one-off job page; the real client's Properties and Jobs tabs; the amended cleaner invoice review page; the amended Xero-pushed review page; the Cleaner invoices list; the cleaner's Pay › Invoices. Flows: Cleaner bills showed the "No account chosen" warning, refused "4 77" (Save disabled), saved 477 and 620, warning gone; the job page's "Attach to client" (opened from the People card link) attached the site to Harbour View Stays and the notice went; the amendment removed the parking line and changed shopping hours 2 → 1.5 (reason typed after Tab from the note field), preview "Total $197.50 → $167.25", saved, notice outcome "In-app notice: in their inbox · Email: failed (email rejected)" (no email provider key in this environment), card, "Lines as amended" and history entry shown; the Xero-pushed invoice showed the panel warning and, after saving, the red "Needs updating in Xero" notice and list pill; the operations manager saw "only an administrator can amend" on it; the cleaner saw "Amended by the office" with both totals, the changes and reason on both invoices, the amended PDF downloaded (200, application/pdf, text "Invoice CL-BRW-1 (amended)" and the amendment block) and another id answered 404.
- **Not inspected in a browser:** the stale-draft and failed-save states of the amend panel and the "I've updated the bill in Xero" form (component and DB tests only); QA inspector invoices page (same component); New job's one-off client warning (unit test only); New invoice's catch-all refusal (DB test only); quote conversion price (unit test only). Real email, Xero, SMS and push were not contacted; no provider credentials exist here. Deployment not done.
- **Fixture rows:** everything the session created was deleted by id: two one-off jobs, their two sites and integration rows, the catch-all client it created, the two walk cleaner invoices and their revisions, two inbox intents with their attempts and inbox rows, two email rows and two direct-send receipts, one booking-confirmation email row (not sent: no provider key), four audit rows, and the `phase3_integrations_v1` setting (absent before). Left: two job numbers consumed (JOB-001003, JOB-001004) and sign-in side effects of the walk's logins.
